# Kerbal first names from Kerbal Space Program (CrewGenerator, en-us), for the optional Character Manager.
# Format: <pool> <name>, one per line. KSP appends the surname "Kerman" and draws a special name 1 time in 20.
#   veteran    the four starting kerbals, hardcoded in KSP, never generated
#   special-m, special-f   real-world and dev references
#   common-m, common-f     the generated pools
# Each pool is sorted and deduplicated (KSP's own lists repeat some names).
